157: +package core
158: +
159: +import (
160: +	"fmt"
161: +	"strings"
162: +
163: +	clabtypes "github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/types"
164: +	"gopkg.in/yaml.v2"
165: +)
166: +
167: +// labRuntimeTopologyWithExpandedMagicVars renders the per-node values that local runtimes
168: +// normally resolve while initializing nodes. Inherited fields are materialized on each node so
169: +// that defaults, kinds, and groups can contain node-scoped variables too.
170: +func (c *CLab) labRuntimeTopologyWithExpandedMagicVars() ([]byte, error) {
171: +	source := &Config{}
172: +	if err := yaml.Unmarshal(c.renderedTopology, source); err != nil {
173: +		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
174: +			"failed to parse rendered topology for magic variable expansion: %w",
175: +			err,
176: +		)
177: +	}
178: +	if source.Topology == nil {
179: +		return c.renderedTopology, nil
180: +	}
181: +
182: +	expanded := &Config{}
183: +	if err := yaml.Unmarshal(c.renderedTopology, expanded); err != nil {
184: +		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
185: +			"failed to copy rendered topology for magic variable expansion: %w",
186: +			err,
187: +		)
188: +	}
189: +
190: +	for nodeName := range source.Topology.Nodes {
191: +		if err := c.expandLabRuntimeNodeMagicVars(
192: +			source.Topology,
193: +			expanded.Topology,
194: +			nodeName,
195: +		); err != nil {
196: +			return nil, err
197: +		}
198: +	}
199: +	clearInheritedLabRuntimeNodeFields(expanded.Topology)
200: +
201: +	var document yaml.MapSlice
202: +	if err := yaml.Unmarshal(c.renderedTopology, &document); err != nil {
203: +		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse rendered topology document: %w", err)
204: +	}
205: +	for i := range document {
206: +		if document[i].Key == "topology" {
207: +			document[i].Value = expanded.Topology
208: +			break
209: +		}
210: +	}
211: +
212: +	result, err := yaml.Marshal(document)
213: +	if err != nil {
214: +		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to render topology with expanded magic variables: %w", err)
215: +	}
216: +
217: +	return result, nil
218: +}
219: +
220: +func (c *CLab) expandLabRuntimeNodeMagicVars(
221: +	source, expanded *clabtypes.Topology,
222: +	nodeName string,
223: +) error {
224: +	node := expanded.Nodes[nodeName]
225: +	if node == nil {
226: +		node = &clabtypes.NodeDefinition{}
227: +		expanded.Nodes[nodeName] = node
228: +	}
229: +
230: +	r := c.magicVarReplacer(nodeName)
231: +	node.StartupConfig = r.Replace(source.GetNodeStartupConfig(nodeName))
232: +
233: +	var err error
234: +	node.Binds, err = source.GetNodeBinds(nodeName)
235: +	if err != nil {
236: +		return fmt.Errorf(
237: +			"failed to resolve binds for node %q during magic variable expansion: %w",
238: +			nodeName,
239: +			err,
240: +		)
241: +	}
242: +	for i := range node.Binds {
243: +		parts := strings.Split(node.Binds[i], ":")
244: +		parts[0] = r.Replace(parts[0])
245: +		node.Binds[i] = strings.Join(parts, ":")
246: +	}
247: +
248: +	node.Volumes, err = source.GetNodeVolumes(nodeName)
249: +	if err != nil {
250: +		return fmt.Errorf(
251: +			"failed to resolve volumes for node %q during magic variable expansion: %w",
252: +			nodeName,
253: +			err,
254: +		)
255: +	}
256: +	for i := range node.Volumes {
257: +		parts := strings.Split(node.Volumes[i], ":")
258: +		if len(parts) == 1 {
259: +			continue
260: +		}
261: +		parts[0] = r.Replace(parts[0])
262: +		node.Volumes[i] = strings.Join(parts, ":")
263: +	}
264: +
265: +	node.Exec = source.GetNodeExec(nodeName)
266: +	if labRuntimeNodeHasStages(source, nodeName) {
267: +		node.Stages, err = source.GetStages(nodeName)
268: +		if err != nil {
269: +			return fmt.Errorf(
270: +				"failed to resolve stages for node %q during magic variable expansion: %w",
271: +				nodeName,
272: +				err,
273: +			)
274: +		}
275: +	}
276: +	nodeConfig := &clabtypes.NodeConfig{
277: +		ShortName: nodeName,
278: +		Exec:      node.Exec,
279: +		Stages:    node.Stages,
280: +	}
281: +	c.processNodeExecs(nodeConfig)
282: +	node.Exec = nodeConfig.Exec
283: +	node.Stages = nodeConfig.Stages
284: +
285: +	entries := c.expandKindSpecificConfigMagicVars(
286: +		source.GetNodeKindSpecificConfig(nodeName), nodeName,
287: +	)
288: +	if len(entries) != 0 {
289: +		node.KindSpecificConfig = make(map[string]any, len(entries))
290: +		for _, entry := range entries {
291: +			node.KindSpecificConfig[entry.Key] = entry.Value
292: +		}
293: +	}
294: +
295: +	return nil
296: +}
297: +
298: +func labRuntimeNodeHasStages(topology *clabtypes.Topology, nodeName string) bool {
299: +	definitions := []*clabtypes.NodeDefinition{
300: +		topology.Defaults,
301: +		topology.GetKind(topology.GetNodeKind(nodeName)),
302: +		topology.GetGroup(topology.GetNodeGroup(nodeName)),
303: +		topology.Nodes[nodeName],
304: +	}
305: +	for _, definition := range definitions {
306: +		if definition != nil && definition.Stages != nil {
307: +			return true
308: +		}
309: +	}
310: +
311: +	return false
312: +}
313: +
314: +// clearInheritedLabRuntimeNodeFields prevents the inherited values from being merged a second
315: +// time after their effective, expanded copies have been placed on every node.
316: +func clearInheritedLabRuntimeNodeFields(topology *clabtypes.Topology) {
317: +	definitions := []*clabtypes.NodeDefinition{topology.Defaults}
318: +	for _, definition := range topology.Kinds {
319: +		definitions = append(definitions, definition)
320: +	}
321: +	for _, definition := range topology.Groups {
322: +		definitions = append(definitions, definition)
323: +	}
324: +
325: +	for _, definition := range definitions {
326: +		if definition == nil {
327: +			continue
328: +		}
329: +		definition.StartupConfig = ""
330: +		definition.Binds = nil
331: +		definition.Volumes = nil
332: +		definition.Exec = nil
333: +		definition.Stages = nil
334: +		definition.KindSpecificConfig = nil
335: +	}
336: +}

<path>/source/standards/karpathy-guidelines.mdc</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: ---
2: description: Behavioral guidelines to reduce common LLM coding mistakes. Use when writing, reviewing, or refactoring code to avoid overcomplication, make surgical changes, surface assumptions, and define verifiable success criteria.
3: alwaysApply: true
4: ---
5: 
6: # Karpathy behavioral guidelines
7: 
8: Behavioral guidelines to reduce common LLM coding mistakes. Merge with project-specific instructions as needed.
9: 
10: **Tradeoff:** These guidelines bias toward caution over speed. For trivial tasks, use judgment.
11: 
12: ## 1. Think Before Coding
13: 
14: **Don't assume. Don't hide confusion. Surface tradeoffs.**
15: 
16: Before implementing:
17: 
18: - State your assumptions explicitly. If uncertain, ask.
19: - If multiple interpretations exist, present them - don't pick silently.
20: - If a simpler approach exists, say so. Push back when warranted.
21: - If something is unclear, stop. Name what's confusing. Ask.
22: 
23: ## 2. Simplicity First
24: 
25: **Minimum code that solves the problem. Nothing speculative.**
26: 
27: - No features beyond what was asked.
28: - No abstractions for single-use code.
29: - No "flexibility" or "configurability" that wasn't requested.
30: - No error handling for impossible scenarios.
31: - If you write 200 lines and it could be 50, rewrite it.
32: 
33: Ask yourself: "Would a senior engineer say this is overcomplicated?" If yes, simplify.
34: 
35: ## 3. Surgical Changes
36: 
37: **Touch only what you must. Clean up only your own mess.**
38: 
39: When editing existing code:
40: 
41: - Don't "improve" adjacent code, comments, or formatting.
42: - Don't refactor things that aren't broken.
43: - Match existing style, even if you'd do it differently.
44: - If you notice unrelated dead code, mention it - don't delete it.
45: 
46: When your changes create orphans:
47: 
48: - Remove imports/variables/functions that YOUR changes made unused.
49: - Don't remove pre-existing dead code unless asked.
50: 
51: The test: Every changed line should trace directly to the user's request.
52: 
53: ## 4. Goal-Driven Execution
54: 
55: **Define success criteria. Loop until verified.**
56: 
57: Transform tasks into verifiable goals:
58: 
59: - "Add validation" → "Write tests for invalid inputs, then make them pass"
60: - "Fix the bug" → "Write a test that reproduces it, then make it pass"
61: - "Refactor X" → "Ensure tests pass before and after"
62: 
63: For multi-step tasks, state a brief plan:
64: 
65: ```
66: 1. [Step] → verify: [check]
67: 2. [Step] → verify: [check]
68: 3. [Step] → verify: [check]
69: ```
70: 
71: Strong success criteria let you loop independently. Weak criteria ("make it work") require constant clarification.
72: 
73: ---
74: 
75: **These guidelines are working if:** fewer unnecessary changes in diffs, fewer rewrites due to overcomplication, and clarifying questions come before implementation rather than after mistakes.

(End of file - total 75 lines)
</content>

Weak criteria (\"make it work\") require constant clarification.\n72: \n73: ---\n74: \n75: **These guidelines are working if:** fewer unnecessary changes in diffs, fewer rewrites due to overcomplication, and clarifying questions come before implementation rather than after mistakes.\n\n(End of file - total 75 lines)\n</content>",

        "text": "---\ndescription: Behavioral guidelines to reduce common LLM coding mistakes. Use when writing, reviewing, or refactoring code to avoid overcomplication, make surgical changes, surface assumptions, and define verifiable success criteria.\nalwaysApply: true\n---\n\n# Karpathy behavioral guidelines\n\nBehavioral guidelines to reduce common LLM coding mistakes. Merge with project-specific instructions as needed.\n\n**Tradeoff:** These guidelines bias toward caution over speed. For trivial tasks, use judgment.\n\n## 1. Think Before Coding\n\n**Don't assume. Don't hide confusion. Surface tradeoffs.**\n\nBefore implementing:\n\n- State your assumptions explicitly. If uncertain, ask.\n- If multiple interpretations exist, present them - don't pick silently.\n- If a simpler approach exists, say so. Push back when warranted.\n- If something is unclear, stop. Name what's confusing. Ask.\n\n## 2. Simplicity First\n\n**Minimum code that solves the problem. Nothing speculative.**\n\n- No features beyond what was asked.\n- No abstractions for single-use code.\n- No \"flexibility\" or \"configurability\" that wasn't requested.\n- No error handling for impossible scenarios.\n- If you write 200 lines and it could be 50, rewrite it.\n\nAsk yourself: \"Would a senior engineer say this is overcomplicated?\" If yes, simplify.\n\n## 3. Surgical Changes\n\n**Touch only what you must. Clean up only your own mess.**\n\nWhen editing existing code:\n\n- Don't \"improve\" adjacent code, comments, or formatting.\n- Don't refactor things that aren't broken.\n- Match existing style, even if you'd do it differently.\n- If you notice unrelated dead code, mention it - don't delete it.\n\nWhen your changes create orphans:\n\n- Remove imports/variables/functions that YOUR changes made unused.\n- Don't remove pre-existing dead code unless asked.\n\nThe test: Every changed line should trace directly to the user's request.\n\n## 4. Goal-Driven Execution\n\n**Define success criteria. Loop until verified.**\n\nTransform tasks into verifiable goals:\n\n- \"Add validation\" → \"Write tests for invalid inputs, then make them pass\"\n- \"Fix the bug\" → \"Write a test that reproduces it, then make it pass\"\n- \"Refactor X\" → \"Ensure tests pass before and after\"\n\nFor multi-step tasks, state a brief plan:\n\n```\n1. [Step] → verify: [check]\n2. [Step] → verify: [check]\n3. [Step] → verify: [check]\n```\n\nStrong success criteria let you loop independently. Weak criteria (\"make it work\") require constant clarification.\n\n---\n\n**These guidelines are working if:** fewer unnecessary changes in diffs, fewer rewrites due to overcomplication, and clarifying questions come before implementation rather than after mistakes.",

<path>/source/standards/ponytail.mdc</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: ---
2: description: Ponytail, lazy senior dev mode. Always pick the simplest solution that works.
3: globs:
4: alwaysApply: true
5: ---
6: 
7: # Ponytail, lazy senior dev mode
8: 
9: You are a lazy senior developer. Lazy means efficient, not careless. The best code is the code never written.
10: 
11: Before writing any code, stop at the first rung that holds:
12: 
13: 1. Does this need to be built at all? (YAGNI)
14: 2. Does it already exist in this codebase? Reuse the helper, util, or pattern that's already here, don't re-write it.
15: 3. Does the standard library already do this? Use it.
16: 4. Does a native platform feature cover it? Use it.
17: 5. Does an already-installed dependency solve it? Use it.
18: 6. Can this be one line? Make it one line.
19: 7. Only then: write the minimum code that works.
20: 
21: The ladder runs after you understand the problem, not instead of it: read the task and the code it touches, trace the real flow end to end, then climb.
22: 
23: Bug fix = root cause, not symptom: a report names a symptom. Grep every caller of the function you touch and fix the shared function once — one guard there is a smaller diff than one per caller, and patching only the path the ticket names leaves a sibling caller still broken.
24: 
25: Rules:
26: 
27: - No abstractions that weren't explicitly requested.
28: - No new dependency if it can be avoided.
29: - No boilerplate nobody asked for.
30: - Deletion over addition. Boring over clever. Fewest files possible.
31: - Shortest working diff wins, but only once you understand the problem. The smallest change in the wrong place isn't lazy, it's a second bug.
32: - Question complex requests: "Do you actually need X, or does Y cover it?"
33: - Pick the edge-case-correct option when two stdlib approaches are the same size, lazy means less code, not the flimsier algorithm.
34: - Mark deliberate simplifications that cut a real corner with a known ceiling (global lock, O(n²) scan, naive heuristic) with a `ponytail:` comment naming the ceiling and upgrade path.
35: 
36: Not lazy about: understanding the problem (read it fully and trace the real flow before picking a rung, a small diff you don't understand is just laziness dressed up as efficiency), input validation at trust boundaries, error handling that prevents data loss, security, accessibility, the calibration real hardware needs (the platform is never the spec ideal, a clock drifts, a sensor reads off), anything explicitly requested. Lazy code without its check is unfinished: non-trivial logic leaves ONE runnable check behind, the smallest thing that fails if the logic breaks (an assert-based demo/self-check or one small test file; no frameworks, no fixtures). Trivial one-liners need no test.

(End of file - total 36 lines)
</content>

1: package core
2: 
3: import (
4: 	"fmt"

7: 	clabtypes "github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/types"

10: 
11: // labRuntimeTopologyWithExpandedMagicVars renders the per-node values that local runtimes
12: // normally resolve while initializing nodes. Inherited fields are materialized on each node so
13: // that defaults, kinds, and groups can contain node-scoped variables too.
14: func (c *CLab) labRuntimeTopologyWithExpandedMagicVars() ([]byte, error) {
15: 	source := &Config{}
16: 	if err := yaml.Unmarshal(c.renderedTopology, source); err != nil {
17: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
18: 			"failed to parse rendered topology for magic variable expansion: %w",
19: 			err,
20: 		)
21: 	}
22: 	if source.Topology == nil {
23: 		return c.renderedTopology, nil
24: 	}
25: 
26: 	expanded := &Config{}
27: 	if err := yaml.Unmarshal(c.renderedTopology, expanded); err != nil {
28: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
29: 			"failed to copy rendered topology for magic variable expansion: %w",
30: 			err,
31: 		)
32: 	}
33: 
34: 	for nodeName := range source.Topology.Nodes {
35: 		if err := c.expandLabRuntimeNodeMagicVars(
36: 			source.Topology,
37: 			expanded.Topology,
38: 			nodeName,
39: 		); err != nil {
40: 			return nil, err
41: 		}
42: 	}
43: 	clearInheritedLabRuntimeNodeFields(expanded.Topology)
44: 
45: 	var document yaml.MapSlice
46: 	if err := yaml.Unmarshal(c.renderedTopology, &document); err != nil {
47: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse rendered topology document: %w", err)
48: 	}
49: 	for i := range document {
50: 		if document[i].Key == "topology" {
51: 			document[i].Value = expanded.Topology
52: 			break
53: 		}
54: 	}
55: 
56: 	result, err := yaml.Marshal(document)
57: 	if err != nil {
58: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to render topology with expanded magic variables: %w", err)
59: 	}
60: 
61: 	return result, nil
62: }
63: 
64: func (c *CLab) expandLabRuntimeNodeMagicVars(
65: 	source, expanded *clabtypes.Topology,
66: 	nodeName string,
67: ) error {
68: 	node := expanded.Nodes[nodeName]
69: 	if node == nil {
70: 		node = &clabtypes.NodeDefinition{}
71: 		expanded.Nodes[nodeName] = node
72: 	}
73: 
74: 	r := c.magicVarReplacer(nodeName)
75: 	node.StartupConfig = r.Replace(source.GetNodeStartupConfig(nodeName))
76: 
77: 	var err error
78: 	node.Binds, err = source.GetNodeBinds(nodeName)
79: 	if err != nil {
80: 		return fmt.Errorf(
81: 			"failed to resolve binds for node %q during magic variable expansion: %w",
82: 			nodeName,
83: 			err,
84: 		)
85: 	}
86: 	for i := range node.Binds {
87: 		parts := strings.Split(node.Binds[i], ":")
88: 		parts[0] = r.Replace(parts[0])
89: 		node.Binds[i] = strings.Join(parts, ":")
90: 	}
91: 
92: 	node.Volumes, err = source.GetNodeVolumes(nodeName)
93: 	if err != nil {
94: 		return fmt.Errorf(
95: 			"failed to resolve volumes for node %q during magic variable expansion: %w",
96: 			nodeName,
97: 			err,
98: 		)
99: 	}
100: 	for i := range node.Volumes {
101: 		parts := strings.Split(node.Volumes[i], ":")
102: 		if len(parts) == 1 {
103: 			continue
104: 		}
105: 		parts[0] = r.Replace(parts[0])
106: 		node.Volumes[i] = strings.Join(parts, ":")
107: 	}
108: 
109: 	node.Exec = source.GetNodeExec(nodeName)
110: 	if labRuntimeNodeHasStages(source, nodeName) {
111: 		node.Stages, err = source.GetStages(nodeName)
112: 		if err != nil {
113: 			return fmt.Errorf(
114: 				"failed to resolve stages for node %q during magic variable expansion: %w",
115: 				nodeName,
116: 				err,
117: 			)
118: 		}
119: 	}
120: 	nodeConfig := &clabtypes.NodeConfig{
121: 		ShortName: nodeName,
122: 		Exec:      node.Exec,
123: 		Stages:    node.Stages,
124: 	}
125: 	c.processNodeExecs(nodeConfig)
126: 	node.Exec = nodeConfig.Exec
127: 	node.Stages = nodeConfig.Stages
128: 
129: 	entries := c.expandKindSpecificConfigMagicVars(
130: 		source.GetNodeKindSpecificConfig(nodeName), nodeName,
131: 	)
132: 	if len(entries) != 0 {
133: 		node.KindSpecificConfig = make(map[string]any, len(entries))
134: 		for _, entry := range entries {
135: 			node.KindSpecificConfig[entry.Key] = entry.Value
136: 		}
137: 	}
138: 
139: 	return nil
140: }
141: 
142: func labRuntimeNodeHasStages(topology *clabtypes.Topology, nodeName string) bool {
143: 	definitions := []*clabtypes.NodeDefinition{
144: 		topology.Defaults,
145: 		topology.GetKind(topology.GetNodeKind(nodeName)),
146: 		topology.GetGroup(topology.GetNodeGroup(nodeName)),
147: 		topology.Nodes[nodeName],
148: 	}
149: 	for _, definition := range definitions {
150: 		if definition != nil && definition.Stages != nil {
151: 			return true
152: 		}
153: 	}
154: 
155: 	return false
156: }
157: 
158: // clearInheritedLabRuntimeNodeFields prevents the inherited values from being merged a second
159: // time after their effective, expanded copies have been placed on every node.
160: func clearInheritedLabRuntimeNodeFields(topology *clabtypes.Topology) {
161: 	definitions := []*clabtypes.NodeDefinition{topology.Defaults}
162: 	for _, definition := range topology.Kinds {
163: 		definitions = append(definitions, definition)
164: 	}
165: 	for _, definition := range topology.Groups {
166: 		definitions = append(definitions, definition)
167: 	}
168: 
169: 	for _, definition := range definitions {
170: 		if definition == nil {
171: 			continue
172: 		}
173: 		definition.StartupConfig = ""
174: 		definition.Binds = nil
175: 		definition.Volumes = nil
176: 		definition.Exec = nil
177: 		definition.Stages = nil
178: 		definition.KindSpecificConfig = nil
179: 	}
180: }

202: // sanitizeLabRuntimeNames aligns the lab and node names with what a lab runtime can name the
203: // objects it creates with. The lab name is renamed here so containerlab and the runtime keep
204: // addressing the lab by the same name; node names are renamed by the runtime itself, where the
205: // topology it hands over is rewritten, and are only reported here.
206: func (c *CLab) sanitizeLabRuntimeNames() error {
207: 	if sanitized := clabnames.SanitizeName(c.Config.Name); sanitized != c.Config.Name {
208: 		if sanitized == "" {
209: 			return fmt.Errorf(
210: 				"lab name %q holds no character a lab runtime object name can be built from",
211: 				c.Config.Name,
212: 			)
213: 		}
214: 
215: 		log.Warn(
216: 			"Lab name cannot name the objects the lab runtime creates and was sanitized",
217: 			"name", c.Config.Name,
218: 			"sanitized", sanitized,
219: 		)
220: 
221: 		c.Config.Name = sanitized
222: 	}
223: 
224: 	if c.Config.Topology == nil {
225: 		return nil
226: 	}
227: 
228: 	nodeNames := make([]string, 0, len(c.Config.Topology.Nodes))
229: 	for nodeName := range c.Config.Topology.Nodes {
230: 		nodeNames = append(nodeNames, nodeName)
231: 	}
232: 
233: 	renames, err := clabnames.SanitizeNodeNames(nodeNames)
234: 	if err != nil {
235: 		return err
236: 	}
237: 	if len(renames) == 0 {
238: 		return nil
239: 	}
240: 
241: 	renamed := make([]string, 0, len(renames))
242: 	for _, nodeName := range slices.Sorted(maps.Keys(renames)) {
243: 		renamed = append(renamed, fmt.Sprintf("%s -> %s", nodeName, renames[nodeName]))
244: 	}
245: 
246: 	log.Warn(
247: 		"Node names cannot name the objects the lab runtime creates and were sanitized",
248: 		"nodes", strings.Join(renamed, ", "),
249: 	)
250: 
251: 	return nil
252: }

254: // ProcessTopoPath takes a topology path, which might be the path to a directory or a file
255: // or stdin or a URL (HTTP/HTTPS/S3) and returns the topology file name if found.
256: func (c *CLab) ProcessTopoPath(path string) (string, error) {
257: 	var file string
258: 
259: 	var err error
260: 
261: 	switch {
262: 	case path == "-" || path == "stdin":
263: 		log.Debugf("interpreting topo %q as stdin", path)
264: 
265: 		file, err = readFromStdin(c.TopoPaths.ClabTmpDir())
266: 		if err != nil {
267: 			return "", err
268: 		}
269: 	// if the path is not a local file and a URL, download the file and store it in the tmp dir
270: 	case !clabutils.FileOrDirExists(path) &&
271: 		clabutils.IsHttpURL(path, true):
272: 		log.Debugf("interpreting topo %q as remote URL", path)
273: 
274: 		file, err = downloadTopoFile(path, c.TopoPaths.ClabTmpDir())
275: 		if err != nil {
276: 			return "", err
277: 		}
278: 	// if the path is an S3 URL, download the file and store it in the tmp dir
279: 	case clabutils.IsS3URL(path):
280: 		log.Debugf("interpreting topo %q as S3 URL", path)
281: 
282: 		file, err = downloadTopoFile(path, c.TopoPaths.ClabTmpDir())
283: 		if err != nil {
284: 			return "", err
285: 		}
286: 
287: 	case path == "":
288: 		return "", fmt.Errorf("provide a path to the clab topology file")
289: 
290: 	default:
291: 		log.Debugf("interpreting topo %q as file path", path)
292: 
293: 		file, err = FindTopoFileByPath(path)
294: 		if err != nil {
295: 			return "", err
296: 		}
297: 	}
298: 
299: 	return file, nil
300: }

Reasoning
Now I need to understand the flow. Key things to check:

1. Where `c.renderedTopology` is set before `prepareLabRuntimeTopology` is called. The diff shows `prepareLabRuntimeTopology` now sets `c.renderedTopology = expandedTopology`. But when is `renderedTopology` parsed (i.e., where does `c.renderedTopology` come from)? Presumably from `parseTopology` in `NewContainerLab` (options). Need to trace `labRuntimeDeployRequest` and how `renderedTopology` is used elsewhere — whether overwriting `c.renderedTopology` affects other consumers, e.g., `ExportTopoData`, config save, `clab inspect` writing the file, etc.

2. The double `yaml.Unmarshal(c.renderedTopology, source)` and `expanded` — identical parsing twice; simplicity issue (P4).

3. The magic variable expansion in binds: `strings.Split(node.Binds[i], ":")` — a Windows path or IPv6 in bind source? Actually the expansion only replaces the first colon-part; if a bind is `c:/path:/target` on Windows... but that's an edge case. More relevant: existing helper may handle this elsewhere. Check how magic vars are normally applied to binds in nodes package — e.g., `nodes.NewNodeConfig` or `types` might have `StringMapSubstituteNodeVars`. Let's search for `magicVarReplacer` usage.

4. `processNodeExecs` — check its signature and semantics. It takes a NodeConfig. Need to check that only ShortName is required for magic var expansion, and whether it mutates.

5. `labRuntimeNodeHasStages` — the guard: only calls `GetStages` if any of definitions has Stages. Why? Maybe `GetStages` panics or returns merged stages... Check.

6. `clearInheritedLabRuntimeNodeFields` — clears Defaults/Kinds/Groups. But what about `topology.Nodes` — not cleared. Also — does it clear Kinds map values which might be shared pointers with `source`? Since `expanded` was unmarshaled separately from the same bytes, they're distinct objects. OK.

Wait — important: `expanded.Topology.Kinds` — types: `Kinds map[string]*NodeDefinition`? And `Groups map[string]*NodeDefinition`? Let me check `types/topology.go`.

7. Big concern: the MapSlice round-trip — `yaml.Unmarshal(c.renderedTopology, &document)` into a `yaml.MapSlice` then replacing the "topology" value with `expanded.Topology` (a struct) and marshaling. Any anchors/aliases in the topology file will be lost/expanded, and comments lost. Does `renderedTopology` already contain comments? Probably it's produced by rendering env vars etc. Let me check where renderedTopology is produced. Actually more subtle: the `Config` struct in core — what is it? Check `core` package's `Config` type. It has `Topology *clabtypes.Topology`. But the topology file has other top-level keys (e.g., `name`, `prefix`, `mgmt`, `deploy`?). The MapSlice approach preserves other top-level keys in order. But there's an issue: if the topology has non-standard keys at top-level (e.g., `settings`?) they survive via MapSlice. Fine.

But wait — a subtle correctness issue: marshaling `expanded.Topology` as the value for "topology" key — yaml.v2 will marshal the struct per its yaml tags. But the original document's `topology` key ordering is preserved via MapSlice. However, the `expanded.Topology` struct unmarshaled from the file and re-marshaled will lose unknown fields within `topology` (fields not in `clabtypes.Topology` struct) — e.g., if topology contains extra keys not modeled. But note: `expanded` was already unmarshaled into `Config`, so unknown fields under topology are already dropped when re-marshaled. But actually before this PR, `renderedTopology` preserved the raw YAML. Does any consumer require unknown topology keys? For the lab-runtime deploy request, `TopologyDefinition` is consumed by the clabernetes runtime... Check what labruntime.DeployRequest does with TopologyDefinition and whether unknown fields matter. Hmm.

Actually wait — is this round-trip lossy in a harmful way? The `types.Topology` struct has yaml tags; unknown keys get dropped silently unless KnownFields is set. E.g., topology keys like `defaults` etc. If a user has custom keys in the topology section (unsupported anyway), it's probably fine.

Bigger concern: the check `if document[i].Key == "topology"` — MapSlice key type is `interface{}` (in yaml.v2, `MapItem.Key interface{}`). Comparing to string works.

8. Ordering concern: `prepareLabRuntimeTopology` sets `c.renderedTopology = expandedTopology` — where else is `renderedTopology` used? Search. If it's used by `ConfigDeploy` or by writing to `clab.yaml`/`ansible-inventory` generation, the expanded version (with per-node inherited values and cleared defaults/kinds/groups) may change other behaviors. E.g., the topology file written to lab dir, or the `Config.Topology` used for API server. Let me grep for `renderedTopology`.

9. `node.Volumes` handling: for binds they always replace parts[0] even if len(parts)==1, but for volumes they `continue` if len(parts)==1. Inconsistent. Also for binds with Windows drive letters (e.g., `C:\path:/target`), splitting on ":" would break, but the pre-existing code elsewhere probably handles binds the same way. Check how the existing node processing handles magic vars in binds — there's likely a `ReplaceAll` in nodes runtime. Let me look at `types/node_definition.go` and how `GetNodeBinds` works.

Also: Windows paths like `C:\Users\...` — split on ":" gives ["C", "\Users\...:/target"] — replacing parts[0]="C" would... magic var replacement on "C" wouldn't match, so benign. OK.

But a genuine issue: for volumes with a `:ro`-style option or named volumes containing colon, replacement of parts[0] is fine.

10. What about `__clabDir__`/`__clabNodeDir__` in exec — `processNodeExecs` handles those. Check `processNodeExecs` in core package — it likely uses `magicVarReplacer`-like substitution with TopoPaths. Actually in nodes package there's `processTopoPathAndSubVars`. Let me look.

Previously, clabernetes probably received the topology with `__clabNodeName__` unexpanded, and the runtime would... 24: 	ctx context.Context,
25: 	options *DeployOptions,
26: ) ([]clabruntime.GenericContainer, error) {
27: 	if len(c.nodeFilter) != 0 {

29: 			c.globalRuntimeName)

37: 		// --reconfigure regenerates the lab from scratch, like removing a local lab
38: 		// directory, so it also deletes the persistent node storage.

40: 			Identity:   c.labRuntimeIdentity,

67: func (c *CLab) labRuntimeDeployRequest() (clablabruntime.DeployRequest, error) {
68: 	if c.Config.Name == "" {
69: 		return clablabruntime.DeployRequest{}, fmt.Errorf("topology name is required")
70: 	}
71: 	if len(c.renderedTopology) == 0 {
72: 		return clablabruntime.DeployRequest{}, fmt.Errorf("rendered topology is empty")
73: 	}
74: 
75: 	topologyFile := ""
76: 	topologyLabDir := ""
77: 	if c.TopoPaths != nil {
78: 		topologyFile = c.TopoPaths.TopologyFilenameAbsPath()
79: 		topologyLabDir = c.TopoPaths.TopologyLabDir()
80: 	}
81: 
82: 	return clablabruntime.DeployRequest{
83: 		Identity:           c.labRuntimeIdentity,
84: 		Name:               c.Config.Name,
85: 		Owner:              c.labOwner(),
86: 		TopologyFile:       topologyFile,
87: 		TopologyLabDir:     topologyLabDir,
88: 		TopologyDefinition: c.renderedTopology,
89: 		Timeout:            c.timeout,
90: 	}, nil
91: }

93: // applyLabRuntimeDeployOptions copies the deploy options that shape remote resources onto the
94: // runtime request in one place, so every path that builds a request agrees on them.
95: func applyLabRuntimeDeployOptions(req *clablabruntime.DeployRequest, options *DeployOptions) {
96: 	if options == nil {
97: 		return
98: 	}
99: 
100: 	req.NoTopologyCR = options.noTopologyCR
101: 	req.ImagePullSecret = options.imagePullSecret
102: 	req.ExposeType = options.exposeType
103: 	req.NoPersistence = options.noPersistence
104: 	req.MaxWorkers = options.maxWorkers
105: }

107: // LabRuntimeManifests renders the remote resources the selected runtime would create for the
108: // topology, shaped by the same deploy options a real deployment honors, without contacting the
109: // runtime's backend.
110: func (c *CLab) LabRuntimeManifests(
111: 	ctx context.Context,
112: 	options *DeployOptions,
113: ) ([]clablabruntime.Manifest, error) {
114: 	if c.LabRuntime == nil {
115: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no lab runtime selected")
116: 	}
117: 
118: 	emitter, ok := c.LabRuntime.(clablabruntime.ManifestEmitter)
119: 	if !ok {
120: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
121: 			"lab runtime %q does not support emitting manifests",
122: 			c.globalRuntimeName,
123: 		)
124: 	}
125: 
126: 	req, err := c.labRuntimeDeployRequest()
127: 	if err != nil {
128: 		return nil, err
129: 	}
130: 	applyLabRuntimeDeployOptions(&req, options)
131: 
132: 	return emitter.Manifests(ctx, req)
133: }

192: // cleanupLabRuntimeLabDir removes the local lab directory for --cleanup parity with local
193: // runtimes. Controller-driven runtimes keep node state remotely, so the directory usually only
194: // exists when a lab was previously deployed locally or configs were copied out.
195: func (c *CLab) cleanupLabRuntimeLabDir() error {
196: 	if c.TopoPaths == nil || !c.TopoPaths.TopologyFileIsSet() {
197: 		return nil
198: 	}
199: 
200: 	labDir := c.TopoPaths.TopologyLabDir()
201: 	if labDir == "" {
202: 		return nil
203: 	}
204: 	if _, err := os.Stat(labDir); os.IsNotExist(err) {
205: 		return nil
206: 	}
207: 
208: 	log.Info("Removing lab directory", "path", labDir)
209: 
210: 	if err := os.RemoveAll(labDir); err != nil {
211: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove lab directory %q: %w", labDir, err)
212: 	}
213: 
214: 	return nil
215: }

252: 		}); err != nil {
253: 			errs = append(errs, err)
254: 		}
255: 	}
256:

259: 			errors.Join(errs...))
260: 	}
261: 
262: 	return nil
263: }

303: func (c *CLab) execWithLabRuntime(
304: 	ctx context.Context,
305: 	cmds []string,
306: 	listOptions ...ListOption,
307: ) (*clabexec.ExecCollection, error) {
308: 	containers, err := c.ListLabRuntimeContainers(ctx, false, listOptions...)
309: 	if err != nil {
310: 		return nil, err
311: 	}
312: 	if len(containers) == 0 {
313: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("filter did not match any containers")
314: 	}
315: 
316: 	var execCmds []*clabexec.ExecCmd
317: 	for _, execCmdStr := range cmds {
318: 		execCmd, err := clabexec.NewExecCmdFromString(execCmdStr)
319: 		if err != nil {
320: 			return nil, err
321: 		}
322: 		execCmds = append(execCmds, execCmd)
323: 	}
324: 
325: 	resultCollection := clabexec.NewExecCollection()
326: 	var execErrors []error
327: 	for idx := range containers {
328: 		namespace, labName, nodeName, err := labRuntimeContainerParts(containers[idx])
329: 		if err != nil {
330: 			log.Warnf("exec target %s is invalid: %v", containers[idx].Names[0], err)
331: 			execErrors = append(execErrors, fmt.Errorf(
332: 				"exec target %s is invalid: %w", containers[idx].Names[0], err))
333: 			continue
334: 		}
335: 
336: 		for _, execCmd := range execCmds {
337: 			result, err := c.LabRuntime.Exec(ctx, clablabruntime.ExecRequest{
338: 				Identity:  c.labRuntimeIdentity,
339: 				Name:      labName,
340: 				Namespace: namespace,
341: 				NodeName:  nodeName,
342: 				Command:   execCmd.GetCmd(),
343: 			})
344: 			if err != nil {
345: 				log.Warnf("exec on %s failed: %v", containers[idx].Names[0], err)
346: 				execErrors = append(execErrors, fmt.Errorf(
347: 					"exec on %s failed: %w", containers[idx].Names[0], err))
348: 				continue
349: 			}
350: 
351: 			resultCollection.Add(containers[idx].Names[0], result)
352: 			if result.GetReturnCode() != 0 {
353: 				execErrors = append(execErrors, fmt.Errorf(
354: 					"exec on %s returned exit code %d",
355: 					containers[idx].Names[0],
356: 					result.GetReturnCode(),
357: 				))
358: 			}
359: 		}
360: 	}
361: 
362: 	return resultCollection, errors.Join(execErrors...)
363: }

418: func (c *CLab) containersFromLabState(
419: 	state *clablabruntime.LabState,
420: ) []clabruntime.GenericContainer {
421: 	if state == nil {
422: 		return nil
423: 	}
424: 
425: 	nodes := state.Nodes
426: 	if len(nodes) == 0 && c.Config.Topology != nil {
427: 		nodeNames := make([]string, 0, len(c.Config.Topology.Nodes))
428: 		for nodeName := range c.Config.Topology.Nodes {
429: 			nodeNames = append(nodeNames, nodeName)
430: 		}
431: 		sort.Strings(nodeNames)
432: 
433: 		nodes = make([]clablabruntime.NodeState, 0, len(nodeNames))
434: 		for _, nodeName := range nodeNames {
435: 			nodes = append(nodes, clablabruntime.NodeState{
436: 				Name:  nodeName,
437: 				Kind:  c.Config.Topology.GetNodeKind(nodeName),
438: 				Image: c.Config.Topology.GetNodeImage(nodeName),
439: 				State: state.State,
440: 				Ready: state.Ready,
441: 			})
442: 		}
443: 	}
444: 
445: 	containers := make([]clabruntime.GenericContainer, 0, len(nodes))
446: 	for _, node := range nodes {
447: 		containers = append(containers, c.containerFromLabNode(state, node))
448: 	}
449: 
450: 	return containers
451: }
452: 
453: // LabRuntimeContainer describes a lab runtime node as a container record, the way inspect
454: // reports it. The events command describes lab runtime nodes with it too.
455: func (c *CLab) LabRuntimeContainer(
456: 	state *clablabruntime.LabState,
457: 	node clablabruntime.NodeState,
458: ) clabruntime.GenericContainer {
459: 	return c.containerFromLabNode(state, node)
460: }
461: 
462: func (c *CLab) containerFromLabNode(
463: 	state *clablabruntime.LabState,
464: 	node clablabruntime.NodeState,
465: ) clabruntime.GenericContainer {
466: 	nodeName := fmt.Sprintf("%s-%s", state.Name, node.Name)
467: 	containerState, containerStatus := labRuntimeContainerState(state, node)
468: 
469: 	topologyNode := node.Name
470: 	if node.TopologyName != "" {
471: 		topologyNode = node.TopologyName
472: 	}
473: 	if c.Config.Topology != nil {
474: 		topologyNode = c.topologyNodeName(topologyNode)
475: 	}
476: 
477: 	labels := map[string]string{
478: 		clabconstants.Containerlab: state.Name,
479: 		clabconstants.NodeName:     topologyNode,
480: 		clabconstants.LongName:     nodeName,
481: 		clabconstants.TopoFile:     labRuntimeTopologyPath(c, state),
482: 	}
483: 
484: 	var image string
485: 	if c.Config.Topology != nil {
486: 		labels[clabconstants.NodeKind] = c.Config.Topology.GetNodeKind(topologyNode)
487: 		image = c.Config.Topology.GetNodeImage(topologyNode)
488: 	}
489: 	if node.Kind != "" {
490: 		labels[clabconstants.NodeKind] = node.Kind
491: 	}
492: 	if node.Image != "" {
493: 		image = node.Image
494: 	}
495: 
496: 	// The node's type and group: from the loaded topology's node, with the kind's defaults the
497: 	// local runtimes report too, otherwise as the topology set them on the runtime's node.
498: 	nodeType, group := node.Type, node.Group
499: 	if topologyNodeConfig, ok := c.Nodes[topologyNode]; ok {
500: 		nodeType, group = topologyNodeConfig.Config().NodeType, topologyNodeConfig.Config().Group
501: 	} else if c.Config.Topology != nil && group == "" {
502: 		group = c.Config.Topology.GetNodeGroup(topologyNode)
503: 	}
504: 	if nodeType != "" {
505: 		labels[clabconstants.NodeType] = nodeType
506: 	}
507: 	if group != "" {
508: 		labels[clabconstants.NodeGroup] = group
509: 	}
510: 
511: 	if state.Owner != "" {
512: 		labels[clabconstants.Owner] = state.Owner
513: 	} else if c.customOwner != "" {
514: 		labels[clabconstants.Owner] = c.customOwner
515: 	}
516: 
517: 	// The ID names the node uniquely across namespaces and labs. It is also the short ID, so
518: 	// inspect reports it as container_id and it matches the events actor ID.
519: 	id := labRuntimeContainerID(state.Namespace, state.Name, node.Name)
520: 
521: 	return clabruntime.GenericContainer{
522: 		Names:           []string{nodeName},
523: 		ID:              id,
524: 		ShortID:         id,
525: 		Image:           image,
526: 		State:           containerState,
527: 		Status:          containerStatus,
528: 		Labels:          labels,
529: 		NetworkName:     state.Namespace,
530: 		NetworkSettings: managementAddress(node),
531: 		Ports:           labRuntimePorts(node.ExposedPorts),
532: 		Workload: &clabtypes.LabRuntimeWorkload{
533: 			Namespace:           state.Namespace,
534: 			Pod:                 node.Pod,
535: 			Container:           node.Container,
536: 			NodeUID:             node.NodeUID,
537: 			ExposedPorts:        node.ExposedPorts,
538: 			LoadBalancerAddress: node.LoadBalancerAddress,
539: 		},
540: 	}
541: }
542: 
543: // labRuntimeContainerState is a lab runtime node's state and status in the vocabulary of the
544: // container runtimes: state created, running or exited; status healthy or "health: starting"
545: // for a running node, as inspect shortens a Docker status line with a healthcheck, Created or
546: // Exited otherwise. A runtime that reports no container state keeps its own state.
547: func labRuntimeContainerState(
548: 	state *clablabruntime.LabState,
549: 	node clablabruntime.NodeState,
550: ) (string, string) {
551: 	switch node.ContainerState {
552: 	case clablabruntime.ContainerStateRunning:
553: 		if node.Health == clablabruntime.ContainerHealthHealthy {
554: 			return node.ContainerState, "healthy"
555: 		}
556: 
557: 		return node.ContainerState, "health: starting"
558: 	case clablabruntime.ContainerStateCreated:
559: 		return node.ContainerState, "Created"
560: 	case clablabruntime.ContainerStateExited:
561: 		return node.ContainerState, "Exited"
562: 	}
563: 
564: 	if node.Ready {
565: 		return clablabruntime.ContainerStateRunning, "healthy"
566: 	}
567: 	if node.State == "" {
568: 		return state.State, ""
569: 	}
570: 
571: 	return node.State, node.State
572: }
573: 
574: // labRuntimePorts reports the ports a lab runtime exposes for a node as the ports a container
575: // exposes without publishing them on the host: the node is reached through the runtime (a
576: // Service, a port-forward), never on a port of this machine.
577: func labRuntimePorts(ports []clablabruntime.ExposedPort) []*clabtypes.GenericPortBinding {
578: 	if len(ports) == 0 {
579: 		return nil
580: 	}
581: 
582: 	bindings := make([]*clabtypes.GenericPortBinding, 0, len(ports))
583: 	for _, port := range ports {
584: 		bindings = append(bindings, &clabtypes.GenericPortBinding{
585: 			ContainerPort: port.Port,
586: 			Protocol:      port.Protocol,
587: 		})
588: 	}
589: 
590: 	return bindings
591: }
592: 
593: // labRuntimeContainerID is the ID of a lab runtime node: <namespace>/<lab>/<node>.
594: func labRuntimeContainerID(namespace, lab, node string) string {
595: 	return fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s/%s", namespace, lab, node)
596: }
597: 
598: // topologyNodeName maps a node name a lab runtime reports back onto the topology node it belongs
599: // to. The runtime renames the nodes Kubernetes cannot carry, so the state it reports does not
600: // always use the name the topology file wrote.
601: func (c *CLab) topologyNodeName(name string) string {
602: 	if c.Config.Topology == nil {
603: 		return name
604: 	}
605: 	if _, ok := c.Config.Topology.Nodes[name]; ok {
606: 		return name
607: 	}
608: 
609: 	for nodeName := range c.Config.Topology.Nodes {
610: 		if clabnames.SanitizeName(nodeName) == name {
611: 			return nodeName
612: 		}
613: 	}
614: 
615: 	return name
616: }
617: 
618: // managementAddress reports the address a user reaches the node at: the LoadBalancer address
619: // when the node is exposed, otherwise the allocated management address.
620: func managementAddress(node clablabruntime.NodeState) clabruntime.GenericMgmtIPs {
621: 	settings := clabruntime.GenericMgmtIPs{}
622: 
623: 	if ip := net.ParseIP(node.LoadBalancerAddress); ip != nil {
624: 		if ip.To4() != nil {
625: 			settings.IPv4addr = node.LoadBalancerAddress
626: 			settings.IPv4pLen = 32
627: 		} else {
628: 			settings.IPv6addr = node.LoadBalancerAddress
629: 			settings.IPv6pLen = 128
630: 		}
631: 	}
632: 
633: 	if settings.IPv4addr == "" {
634: 		settings.IPv4addr, settings.IPv4pLen = splitManagementCIDR(node.MgmtIPv4Address)
635: 	}
636: 	if settings.IPv6addr == "" {
637: 		settings.IPv6addr, settings.IPv6pLen = splitManagementCIDR(node.MgmtIPv6Address)
638: 	}
639: 
640: 	return settings
641: }
642: 
643: func splitManagementCIDR(cidr string) (string, int) {
644: 	if cidr == "" {
645: 		return "", 0
646: 	}
647: 
648: 	ip, network, err := net.ParseCIDR(cidr)
649: 	if err != nil {
650: 		if net.ParseIP(cidr) != nil {
651: 			return cidr, 0
652: 		}
653: 
654: 		return "", 0
655: 	}
656: 
657: 	prefixLen, _ := network.Mask.Size()
658: 
659: 	return ip.String(), prefixLen
660: }
661: 
662: func labRuntimeTopologyPath(c *CLab, state *clablabruntime.LabState) string {
663: 	if c.TopoPaths.TopologyFileIsSet() {
664: 		return c.TopoPaths.TopologyFilenameAbsPath()
665: 	}
666: 
667: 	if state.TopologyPath != "" {
668: 		return state.TopologyPath
669: 	}
670: 
671: 	return fmt.Sprintf("k8s://%s/topologies/%s", state.Namespace, state.Name)
672: }
673: 
674: func labRuntimeContainerParts(c clabruntime.GenericContainer) (string, string, string, error) {
675: 	parts := strings.Split(c.ID, "/")
676: 	if len(parts) != 3 {
677: 		return "", "", "", fmt.Errorf("expected namespace/lab/node id, got %q", c.ID)
678: 	}
679: 
680: 	return parts[0], parts[1], parts[2], nil
681: }
682: 
683: func filterLabRuntimeContainers(
684: 	containers []clabruntime.GenericContainer,
685: 	filters []*clabtypes.GenericFilter,
686: ) []clabruntime.GenericContainer {
687: 	if len(filters) == 0 {
688: 		return containers
689: 	}
690: 
691: 	filtered := make([]clabruntime.GenericContainer, 0, len(containers))
692: 	for _, container := range containers {
693: 		if labRuntimeContainerMatches(container, filters) {
694: 			filtered = append(filtered, container)
695: 		}
696: 	}
697: 
698: 	return filtered
699: }
700: 
701: func labRuntimeContainerMatches(
702: 	container clabruntime.GenericContainer,
703: 	filters []*clabtypes.GenericFilter,
704: ) bool {
705: 	for _, filter := range filters {
706: 		switch filter.FilterType {
707: 		case "name":
708: 			if !labRuntimeNameMatches(container.Names, filter.Match) {
709: 				return false
710: 			}
711: 		case "id":
712: 			if container.ID != filter.Match && !strings.HasPrefix(container.ID, filter.Match) {
713: 				return false
714: 			}
715: 		case "label":
716: 			if !labRuntimeLabelMatches(container.Labels, filter) {
717: 				return false
718: 			}
719: 		}
720: 	}
721: 
722: 	return true
723: }
724: 
725: func labRuntimeNameMatches(names []string, match string) bool {
726: 	for _, name := range names {
727: 		if name == match || strings.Contains(name, match) {
728: 			return true
729: 		}
730: 	}
731: 
732: 	return false
733: }
734: 
735: func labRuntimeLabelMatches(labels map[string]string, filter *clabtypes.GenericFilter) bool {
736: 	value, ok := labels[filter.Field]
737: 
738: 	switch filter.Operator {
739: 	case "exists":
740: 		return ok
741: 	case "!=":
742: 		return !ok || value != filter.Match
743: 	default:
744: 		return ok && value == filter.Match
745: 	}
746: }
747: 
748: func (c *CLab) copyLabRuntimeSavedFiles(
749: 	result *clablabruntime.SaveResult,
750: 	dstRoot string,
751: ) error {
752: 	for _, file := range result.Files {
753: 		if file.NodeName == "" || file.Name == "" {
754: 			continue
755: 		}
756: 
757: 		relPath, ok := cleanLabRuntimeSavedPath(file.Name)
758: 		if !ok {
759: 			return fmt.Errorf("refusing to copy unsafe saved config path %q", file.Name)
760: 		}
761: 
762: 		nodeDstDir := filepath.Join(dstRoot, file.NodeName)
763: 		dstPath := filepath.Join(nodeDstDir, relPath)
764: 		if err := os.MkdirAll(
765: 			filepath.Dir(dstPath),
766: 			clabconstants.PermissionsDirDefault,
767: 		); err != nil {
768: 			return fmt.Errorf("failed to create save dst directory %q: %w",
769: 				filepath.Dir(dstPath), err)
770: 		}
771: 
772: 		if file.LinkTarget != "" {
773: 			linkTarget, ok := cleanLabRuntimeSavedLinkTarget(file.LinkTarget)
774: 			if !ok {
775: 				return fmt.Errorf("refusing to create unsafe saved config symlink %q -> %q",
776: 					file.Name, file.LinkTarget)
777: 			}
778: 
779: 			_ = os.Remove(dstPath)
780: 			if err := os.Symlink(linkTarget, dstPath); err != nil {
781: 				return fmt.Errorf("failed to create symlink %q -> %q: %w",
782: 					dstPath, linkTarget, err)
783: 			}
784: 
785: 			continue
786: 		}
787: 
788: 		mode := os.FileMode(file.Mode)
789: 		if mode == 0 {
790: 			mode = clabconstants.PermissionsFileDefault
791: 		}
792: 
793: 		if err := os.WriteFile(dstPath, file.Data, mode); err != nil {
794: 			return fmt.Errorf("failed to write saved config %q: %w", dstPath, err)
795: 		}
796: 
797: 		log.Info(
798: 			"copied saved config",
799: 			"node", file.NodeName,
800: 			"dst", dstPath,
801: 		)
802: 	}
803: 
804: 	return nil
805: }
806: 
807: func cleanLabRuntimeSavedPath(name string) (string, bool) {
808: 	cleaned := filepath.Clean(name)
809: 	if cleaned == "." || filepath.IsAbs(cleaned) ||
810: 		strings.HasPrefix(cleaned, ".."+string(filepath.Separator)) ||
811: 		cleaned == ".." {
812: 		return "", false
813: 	}
814: 
815: 	return cleaned, true
816: }
817: 
818: func cleanLabRuntimeSavedLinkTarget(target string) (string, bool) {
819: 	cleaned := filepath.Clean(target)
820: 	if filepath.IsAbs(cleaned) ||
821: 		strings.HasPrefix(cleaned, ".."+string(filepath.Separator)) ||
822: 		cleaned == ".." {
823: 		return "", false
824: 	}
825: 
826: 	return cleaned, true
827: }

\n574: // labRuntimePorts reports the ports a lab runtime exposes for a node as the ports a container\n575: // exposes without publishing them on the host: the node is reached through the runtime (a\n576: // Service, a port-forward), never on a port of this machine.\n577: func labRuntimePorts(ports []clablabruntime.ExposedPort) []*clabtypes.GenericPortBinding {\n578: \tif len(ports) == 0 []string, match string) bool {\n726: \tfor _, name := range names {\n727: \t\tif name == match || strings.Contains(name, match) {\n728: \t\t\treturn true\n729: \t\t}\n730: \t}\n731: \n732: \treturn false\n733: }\n734: \n735: func labRuntimeLabelMatches(labels map[string]string, filter *clabtypes.GenericFilter) bool {\n736: \tvalue, ok := labels[filter.Field]\n737: \n738: \tswitch filter.Operator {\n739: \tcase \"exists\":\n740: \t\treturn ok\n741: \tcase \"!=\":\n742: \t\treturn !ok || value != filter.Match\n743: \tdefault:\n744: \t\treturn ok && value == filter.Match\n745: \t}\n746: }\n747: \n748: func (c *CLab) copyLabRuntimeSavedFiles(\n749: \tresult *clablabruntime.SaveResult,\n750: \tdstRoot string,\n751: ) error {\n752: \tfor _, file := range result.Files {\n753: \t\tif *clablabruntime.LabState,\n) []clabruntime.GenericContainer {\n\tif state == nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tnodes := state.Nodes\n\tif len(nodes) == 0 && c.Config.Topology != nil {\n\t\tnodeNames := make([]string, 0, len(c.Config.Topology.Nodes))\n\t\tfor nodeName := range c.Config.Topology.Nodes {\n\t\t\tnodeNames = append(nodeNames, nodeName)\n\t\t}\n\t\tsort.Strings(nodeNames)\n\n\t\tnodes = make([]clablabruntime.NodeState, 0, len(nodeNames))\n\t\tfor _, nodeName := range nodeNames {\n\t\t\tnodes = append(nodes, clablabruntime.NodeState{\n\t\t\t\tName:  nodeName,\n\t\t\t\tKind:  c.Config.Topology.GetNodeKind(nodeName),\n\t\t\t\tImage: c.Config.Topology.GetNodeImage(nodeName),\n\t\t\t\tState: state.State,\n\t\t\t\tReady: state.Ready,\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tcontainers := make([]clabruntime.GenericContainer, 0, len(nodes))\n\tfor _, node := range nodes {\n\t\tcontainers = append(containers, c.containerFromLabNode(state, node))\n\t}\n\n\treturn containers\n}\n\n// LabRuntimeContainer describes a lab runtime node as a container record, the way inspect\n// reports it. The events command describes lab runtime nodes with it too.\nfunc (c *CLab) LabRuntimeContainer(\n\tstate *clablabruntime.LabState,\n\tnode clablabruntime.NodeState,\n) clabruntime.GenericContainer {\n\treturn c.containerFromLabNode(state, node)\n}\n\nfunc (c *CLab) containerFromLabNode(\n\tstate *clablabruntime.LabState,\n\tnode clablabruntime.NodeState,\n) clabruntime.GenericContainer {\n\tnodeName := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s-%s\", state.Name, node.Name)\n\tcontainerState, containerStatus := labRuntimeContainerState(state, node)\n\n\ttopologyNode := node.Name\n\tif node.TopologyName != \"\" {\n\t\ttopologyNode = node.TopologyName\n\t}\n\tif c.Config.Topology != nil {\n\t\ttopologyNode = c.topologyNodeName(topologyNode)\n\t}\n\n\tlabels := map[string]string{\n\t\tclabconstants.Containerlab: state.Name,\n\t\tclabconstants.NodeName:     topologyNode,\n\t\tclabconstants.LongName:     nodeName,\n\t\tclabconstants.TopoFile:     labRuntimeTopologyPath(c, state),\n\t}\n\n\tvar image string\n\tif c.Config.Topology != nil {\n\t\tlabels[clabconstants.NodeKind] = c.Config.Topology.GetNodeKind(topologyNode)\n\t\timage = c.Config.Topology.GetNodeImage(topologyNode)\n\t}\n\tif node.Kind != \"\" {\n\t\tlabels[clabconstants.NodeKind] = node.Kind\n\t}\n\tif node.Image != \"\" {\n\t\timage = node.Image\n\t}\n\n\t// The node's type and group: from the loaded topology's node, with the kind's defaults the\n\t// local runtimes report too, otherwise as the topology set them on the runtime's node.\n\tnodeType, group := node.Type, node.Group\n\tif topologyNodeConfig, ok := c.Nodes[topologyNode]; ok {\n\t\tnodeType, group = topologyNodeConfig.Config().NodeType, topologyNodeConfig.Config().Group\n\t} else if c.Config.Topology != nil && group == \"\" {\n\t\tgroup = c.Config.Topology.GetNodeGroup(topologyNode)\n\t}\n\tif nodeType != \"\" {\n\t\tlabels[clabconstants.NodeType] = nodeType\n\t}\n\tif group != \"\" {\n\t\tlabels[clabconstants.NodeGroup] = group\n\t}\n\n\tif state.Owner != \"\" {\n\t\tlabels[clabconstants.Owner] = state.Owner\n\t} else if c.customOwner != \"\" {\n\t\tlabels[clabconstants.Owner] = c.customOwner\n\t}\n\n\t// The ID names the node uniquely across namespaces and labs. It is also the short ID, so\n\t// inspect reports it as container_id and it matches the events actor ID.\n\tid := labRuntimeContainerID(state.Namespace, state.Name, node.Name)\n\n\treturn clabruntime.GenericContainer{\n\t\tNames:           []string{nodeName},\n\t\tID:              id,\n\t\tShortID:         id,\n\t\tImage:           image,\n\t\tState:           containerState,\n\t\tStatus:          containerStatus,\n\t\tLabels:          labels,\n\t\tNetworkName:     state.Namespace,\n\t\tNetworkSettings: managementAddress(node),\n\t\tPorts:           labRuntimePorts(node.ExposedPorts),\n\t\tWorkload: &clabtypes.LabRuntimeWorkload{\n\t\t\tNamespace:           state.Namespace,\n\t\t\tPod:                 node.Pod,\n\t\t\tContainer:           node.Container,\n\t\t\tNodeUID:             node.NodeUID,\n\t\t\tExposedPorts:        node.ExposedPorts,\n\t\t\tLoadBalancerAddress: node.LoadBalancerAddress,\n\t\t},\n\t}\n}\n\n// labRuntimeContainerState is a lab runtime node's state and status in the vocabulary of the\n// container runtimes: state created, running or exited; status healthy or \"health: starting\"\n// for a running node, as inspect shortens a Docker status line with a healthcheck, Created or\n// Exited otherwise. A runtime that reports no container state keeps its own state.\nfunc labRuntimeContainerState(\n\tstate *clablabruntime.LabState,\n\tnode clablabruntime.NodeState,\n) (string, string) {\n\tswitch node.ContainerState {\n\tcase clablabruntime.ContainerStateRunning:\n\t\tif node.Health == clablabruntime.ContainerHealthHealthy {\n\t\t\treturn node.ContainerState, \"healthy\"\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn node.ContainerState, \"health: starting\"\n\tcase clablabruntime.ContainerStateCreated:\n\t\treturn node.ContainerState, \"Created\"\n\tcase clablabruntime.ContainerStateExited:\n\t\treturn node.ContainerState, \"Exited\"\n\t}\n\n\tif node.Ready {\n\t\treturn clablabruntime.ContainerStateRunning, \"healthy\"\n\t}\n\tif node.State == \"\" {\n\t\treturn state.State, \"\"\n\t}\n\n\treturn node.State, node.State\n}\n\n// labRuntimePorts reports the ports a lab runtime exposes for a node as the ports a container\n// exposes without publishing them on the host: the node is reached through the runtime (a\n// Service, a port-forward), never on a port of this machine.\nfunc labRuntimePorts(ports []clablabruntime.ExposedPort) []*clabtypes.GenericPortBinding {\n\tif len(ports) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tbindings := make([]*clabtypes.GenericPortBinding, 0, len(ports))\n\tfor _, port := range ports {\n\t\tbindings = append(bindings, &clabtypes.GenericPortBinding{\n\t\t\tContainerPort: port.Port,\n\t\t\tProtocol:      port.Protocol,\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\treturn bindings\n}\n\n// labRuntimeContainerID is the ID of a lab runtime node: <namespace>/<lab>/<node>.\nfunc labRuntimeContainerID(namespace, lab, node string) string {\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/%s/%s\", namespace, lab, node)\n}\n\n// topologyNodeName maps a node name a lab runtime reports back onto the topology node it belongs\n// to. The runtime renames the nodes Kubernetes cannot carry, so the state it reports does not\n// always use the name the topology file wrote.\nfunc (c *CLab) topologyNodeName(name string) string {\n\tif {\n\tfor _, name := range names {\n\t\tif name == match || strings.Contains(name, match) {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn false\n}\n\nfunc labRuntimeLabelMatches(labels map[string]string, 828: func (c *CLab) resolveVolumePaths(volumes []string, nodeName string) error {
829: 	// checks are skipped when, for example, the destroy operation is run
830: 	if !c.checkBindsPaths {

step-start
{
  "type": "step-start",

  "messageID": "msg_1e01e4f550016DBToeF2OkhCEb"
}
Reasoning
The binds handling in `expandLabRuntimeNodeMagicVars` mirrors `resolveBindPaths`/`resolveVolumePaths` splitting patterns. Note in `resolveBindPaths`, binds[0] element is replaced and resolved to absolute path relative to topology dir. In the new code, `node.Binds` uses raw source binds and only replaces magic vars in parts[0] — no path resolution to abs path, no `~` expansion. Is that OK for clabernetes? In clabernetes, binds paths are relative to... hmm. The clabernetes runtime resolves paths relative to the topology lab dir presumably. This is a design decision; not necessarily a defect.

But an important issue: binds may contain Windows-style absolute paths like `C:\foo:/target` where splitting on ":" and replacing parts[0]="C" is benign. But what about a bind source containing a magic var AND the target side also containing magic vars? Existing behavior only replaces in the host path (elems[0]) — consistent.

Another key check: the `GetNodeBinds` semantics — check `types/topology.go` for `GetNodeBinds`, `GetNodeVolumes`, `GetStages`, `GetNodeExec`, `GetNodeKindSpecificConfig`, `GetNodeStartupConfig`. Also check how magic vars are applied in clabernetes runtime (if there's an existing expansion there that's now redundant/duplicated). Let me grep for `__clabNodeName__` across the repo.

260: 			Defaults: config.Topology.Defaults,
261: 			Kinds:    config.Topology.Kinds,
262: 			Nodes:    config.Topology.Nodes,
263: 			Groups:   config.Topology.Groups,
264: 			Links:    links,
265: 		}
266: 	}
267: 
268: 	return yaml.Marshal(rendered)
269: }
270: 
271: func clabernetesBriefLinks(
272: 	links []*clablinks.LinkDefinition,
273: 	mgmtNetwork string,
274: ) ([]*clablinks.LinkBriefRaw, error) {
275: 	if len(links) == 0 {
276: 		return nil, nil
277: 	}
278: 
279: 	briefLinks := make([]*clablinks.LinkBriefRaw, 0, len(links))
280: 	for _, link := range links {
281: 		if link == nil || link.Link == nil {
282: 			continue
283: 		}
284: 
285: 		brief, err := clabernetesBriefLink(link, mgmtNetwork)
286: 		if err != nil {
287: 			return nil, err
288: 		}
289: 		briefLinks = append(briefLinks, brief)
290: 	}
291: 
292: 	return briefLinks, nil
293: }
294: 
295: func clabernetesBriefLink(
296: 	link *clablinks.LinkDefinition,
297: 	mgmtNetwork string,
298: ) (*clablinks.LinkBriefRaw, error) {
299: 	switch raw := link.Link.(type) {
300: 	case *clablinks.LinkBriefRaw:
301: 		return raw, nil
302: 	case *clablinks.LinkVEthRaw:
303: 		return raw.ToLinkBriefRaw(), nil
304: 	case *clablinks.LinkHostRaw:
305: 		return raw.ToLinkBriefRaw(), nil
306: 	case *clablinks.LinkMgmtNetRaw:
307: 		// the brief form has no network field, it always attaches to the default
308: 		// management network
309: 		if raw.Network != "" && raw.Network != mgmtNetwork {
310: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf(
311: 				"clabernetes runtime supports mgmt-net links to the default management "+
312: 					"network %q only, not to %q",
313: 				mgmtNetwork,
314: 				raw.Network,
315: 			)
316: 		}
317: 		return raw.ToLinkBriefRaw(), nil
318: 	case *clablinks.LinkMacVlanRaw:
319: 		return raw.ToLinkBriefRaw(), nil
320: 	default:
321: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
322: 			"failed to render clabernetes-compatible brief link for %s link",
323: 			link.Link.GetType(),
324: 		)
325: 	}
326: }
327: 
328: // defaultMgmtNetwork returns the name of the management network of the topology,
329: // falling back to the name containerlab uses when the topology does not set one.
330: func defaultMgmtNetwork(mgmt *clabtypes.MgmtNet) string {
331: 	if mgmt == nil || mgmt.Network == "" {
332: 		return clabconstants.DefaultMgmtNetworkName
333: 	}
334: 	return mgmt.Network
335: }
336: 
337: func clabernetesNamingMode(config *clabRuntimeConfig) string {
338: 	if config == nil || config.Prefix == nil || *config.Prefix != "" {
339: 		return ""
340: 	}
341: 
342: 	return clabernetesNamingNonPrefixed
343: }
344: 
345: func stageStartupConfig(
346: 	ctx context.Context,
347: 	config *clabRuntimeConfig,
348: 	topologyName,
349: 	nodeName,
350: 	topologyFileDir,
351: 	topologyLabDir string,
352: 	configMaps map[string]*stagedConfigMap,
353: 	definitionChanged *bool,
354: ) error {
355: 	startupConfig := config.Topology.GetNodeStartupConfig(nodeName)
356: 	if startupConfig == "" {
357: 		return nil
358: 	}
359: 
360: 	if strings.Contains(startupConfig, "\n") {
361: 		nodeDefinition := config.Topology.Nodes[nodeName]
362: 		if nodeDefinition == nil {
363: 			nodeDefinition = &clabtypes.NodeDefinition{}
364: 			config.Topology.Nodes[nodeName] = nodeDefinition
365: 		}
366: 		nodeDefinition.StartupConfig = inlineStartupConfigMountPath
367: 		*definitionChanged = true
368: 
369: 		return stageConfigMapFile(
370: 			configMaps,
371: 			topologyName,
372: 			"inline-startup-config/"+nodeName,
373: 			nodeName,
374: 			inlineStartupConfigMountPath,
375: 			fileModeRead,
376: 			[]byte(startupConfig),
377: 		)
378: 	}
379: 
380: 	resolvedStartupConfig, files, err := resolveNodeFileReference(
381: 		ctx, startupConfig, "startup-config", nodeName, topologyFileDir, topologyLabDir,
382: 	)
383: 	if err != nil {
384: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed staging startup-config for node %q: %w", nodeName, err)
385: 	}
386: 
387: 	if resolvedStartupConfig != startupConfig {
388: 		nodeDefinition := config.Topology.Nodes[nodeName]
389: 		if nodeDefinition == nil {
390: 			nodeDefinition = &clabtypes.NodeDefinition{}
391: 			config.Topology.Nodes[nodeName] = nodeDefinition
392: 		}
393: 		nodeDefinition.StartupConfig = resolvedStartupConfig
394: 		*definitionChanged = true
395: 	}
396: 
397: 	for _, file := range files {
398: 		if err := stageLocalFile(configMaps, topologyName, nodeName, file); err != nil {
399: 			return err
400: 		}
401: 	}
402: 
403: 	return nil
404: }
405: 
406: func stageLicenseFile(
407: 	ctx context.Context,
408: 	config *clabRuntimeConfig,
409: 	topologyName,
410: 	nodeName,
411: 	topologyFileDir,
412: 	topologyLabDir string,
413: 	configMaps map[string]*stagedConfigMap,
414: 	definitionChanged *bool,
415: ) error {
416: 	license := config.Topology.GetNodeLicense(nodeName)
417: 	if license == "" {
418: 		return nil
419: 	}
420: 
421: 	resolvedLicense, files, err := resolveNodeFileReference(
422: 		ctx, license, "license", nodeName, topologyFileDir, topologyLabDir,
423: 	)
424: 	if err != nil {
425: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed staging license for node %q: %w", nodeName, err)
426: 	}
427: 	if resolvedLicense != license {
428: 		nodeDefinition := config.Topology.Nodes[nodeName]
429: 		if nodeDefinition == nil {
430: 			nodeDefinition = &clabtypes.NodeDefinition{}
431: 			config.Topology.Nodes[nodeName] = nodeDefinition
432: 		}
433: 		nodeDefinition.License = resolvedLicense
434: 		*definitionChanged = true
435: 	}
436: 	for _, file := range files {
437: 		if err := stageLocalFile(configMaps, topologyName, nodeName, file); err != nil {
438: 			return err
439: 		}
440: 	}
441: 
442: 	return nil
443: }
444: 
445: // resolveNodeFileReference uses the shared downloader for every supported URL scheme and
446: // preserves the filename (including .partial) that node implementations use to select formats.
447: func resolveNodeFileReference(
448: 	ctx context.Context,
449: 	sourcePath, field, nodeName, topologyFileDir, topologyLabDir string,
450: ) (string, []stagedLocalFile, error) {
451: 	sourcePath = filepath.ToSlash(replaceClabPathVariables(sourcePath, nodeName, topologyLabDir))
452: 	if strings.Contains(sourcePath, "\n") {
453: 		mountPath := filepath.Join("/clabernetes", nodeName, field, "embedded.lic")
454: 		return mountPath, []stagedLocalFile{{
455: 			filePath: mountPath, mode: fileModeRead, content: []byte(sourcePath),
456: 		}}, nil
457: 	}
458: 	if !clabutils.IsDownloadableURL(sourcePath) {
459: 		files, err := resolveLocalFiles(sourcePath, nodeName, topologyFileDir, topologyLabDir)
460: 		return sourcePath, files, err
461: 	}
462: 
463: 	file, err := os.CreateTemp("", "clab-c9s-download-*")
464: 	if err != nil {
465: 		return "", nil, err
466: 	}
467: 	defer os.Remove(file.Name())
468: 	defer file.Close()
469: 	if err := clabutils.CopyFileContents(ctx, sourcePath, file); err != nil {
470: 		return "", nil, err
471: 	}
472: 	info, err := file.Stat()
473: 	if err != nil {
474: 		return "", nil, err
475: 	}
476: 	mountPath := filepath.Join("/clabernetes", nodeName, field,
477: 		filepath.Base(clabutils.FilenameForURL(ctx, sourcePath)))
478: 	staged, err := loadStagedLocalFile(mountPath, file.Name(), info)
479: 	if err != nil {
480: 		return "", nil, err
481: 	}
482: 
483: 	return mountPath, []stagedLocalFile{staged}, nil
484: }
485: 
486: func stageBindFiles(
487: 	config *clabRuntimeConfig,
488: 	topologyName,
489: 	nodeName,
490: 	topologyFileDir,
491: 	topologyLabDir string,
492: 	configMaps map[string]*stagedConfigMap,
493: ) error {
494: 	binds, err := config.Topology.GetNodeBinds(nodeName)
495: 	if err != nil {
496: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed parsing bind mounts for node %q: %w", nodeName, err)
497: 	}
498: 	if len(binds) == 0 {
499: 		return nil
500: 	}
501: 
502: 	for _, bind := range binds {
503: 		parsedBind, err := clabtypes.NewBindFromString(bind)
504: 		if err != nil {
505: 			return fmt.Errorf("failed parsing bind %q for node %q: %w", bind, nodeName, err)
506: 		}
507: 		if parsedBind.Src() == "" {
508: 			continue
509: 		}
510: 
511: 		if err := stageSourcePathIntoConfigMaps(
512: 			configMaps,
513: 			topologyName,
514: 			parsedBind.Src(),
515: 			nodeName,
516: 			topologyFileDir,
517: 			topologyLabDir,
518: 		); err != nil {
519: 			return err
520: 		}
521: 	}
522: 
523: 	return nil
524: }
525: 
526: // stageAdditionalNodeFiles covers path-bearing fields that containerlab normally resolves on
527: // the machine running the CLI. A device pod cannot see those paths unless the adapter copies
528: // them into the node's ConfigMap-backed file projection first.
529: func stageAdditionalNodeFiles(
530: 	config *clabRuntimeConfig,
531: 	topologyName,
532: 	nodeName,
533: 	topologyFileDir,
534: 	topologyLabDir string,
535: 	configMaps map[string]*stagedConfigMap,
536: ) error {
537: 	pathsByField := map[string][]string{
538: 		"env-files": config.Topology.GetNodeEnvFiles(nodeName),
539: 	}
540: 
541: 	// kind-specific config key whose values are paths that a device pod cannot
542: 	// see unless they are staged into the node's ConfigMaps first.
543: 	pathFields := map[string]bool{
544: 		"copy-to-flash": true,
545: 	}
546: 
547: 	for _, entry := range config.Topology.GetNodeKindSpecificConfig(nodeName) {
548: 		if !pathFields[entry.Key] {
549: 			continue
550: 		}
551: 
552: 		paths, ok := entry.Value.([]any)
553: 		if !ok {
554: 			continue
555: 		}
556: 
557: 		pathsByField[entry.Key] = make([]string, 0, len(paths))
558: 		for _, p := range paths {
559: 			if s, ok := p.(string); ok {
560: 				pathsByField[entry.Key] = append(pathsByField[entry.Key], s)
561: 			}
562: 		}
563: 	}
564: 
565: 	fieldNames := make([]string, 0, len(pathsByField))
566: 	for fieldName := range pathsByField {
567: 		fieldNames = append(fieldNames, fieldName)
568: 	}
569: 	sort.Strings(fieldNames)
570: 
571: 	for _, fieldName := range fieldNames {
572: 		for _, sourcePath := range pathsByField[fieldName] {
573: 			if strings.TrimSpace(sourcePath) == "" {
574: 				continue
575: 			}
576: 
577: 			if err := stageSourcePathIntoConfigMaps(
578: 				configMaps,
579: 				topologyName,
580: 				sourcePath,
581: 				nodeName,
582: 				topologyFileDir,
583: 				topologyLabDir,
584: 			); err != nil {
585: 				return fmt.Errorf(
586: 					"failed staging %s path %q for node %q: %w",
587: 					fieldName,
588: 					sourcePath,
589: 					nodeName,
590: 					err,
591: 				)
592: 			}
593: 		}
594: 	}
595: 
596: 	return nil
597: }
598: 
599: func stageSourcePathIntoConfigMaps(
600: 	configMaps map[string]*stagedConfigMap,
601: 	topologyName,
602: 	sourcePath,
603: 	nodeName,
604: 	topologyFileDir,
605: 	topologyLabDir string,
606: ) error {
607: 	files, err := resolveLocalFiles(sourcePath, nodeName, topologyFileDir, topologyLabDir)
608: 	if err != nil {
609: 		return fmt.Errorf(
610: 			"failed staging source path %q for node %q: %w",
611: 			sourcePath,
612: 			nodeName,
613: 			err,
614: 		)
615: 	}
616: 
617: 	for _, file := range files {
618: 		if err := stageLocalFile(configMaps, topologyName, nodeName, file); err != nil {
619: 			return err
620: 		}
621: 	}
622: 
623: 	return nil
624: }
625: 
626: func resolveLocalFiles(
627: 	sourcePath,
628: 	nodeName,
629: 	topologyFileDir,
630: 	topologyLabDir string,
631: ) ([]stagedLocalFile, error) {
632: 	displayPath := replaceClabPathVariables(sourcePath, nodeName, topologyLabDir)
633: 	resolvedPath := clabutils.ResolvePath(displayPath, topologyFileDir)
634: 
635: 	fileInfo, err := os.Stat(resolvedPath)
636: 	if err != nil {
637: 		return nil, err
638: 	}
639: 
640: 	if !fileInfo.IsDir() {
641: 		file, err := loadStagedLocalFile(displayPath, resolvedPath, fileInfo)
642: 		if err != nil {
643: 			return nil, err
644: 		}
645: 
646: 		return []stagedLocalFile{file}, nil
647: 	}
648: 
649: 	files := []stagedLocalFile{}
650: 	err = filepath.WalkDir(resolvedPath, func(path string, d os.DirEntry, walkErr error) error {
651: 		if walkErr != nil {
652: 			return walkErr
653: 		}
654: 		if d.IsDir() {
655: 			return nil
656: 		}
657: 
658: 		fileInfo, err := d.Info()
659: 		if err != nil {
660: 			return err
661: 		}
662: 
663: 		relativePath, err := filepath.Rel(resolvedPath, path)
664: 		if err != nil {
665: 			return err
666: 		}
667: 
668: 		file, err := loadStagedLocalFile(
669: 			filepath.ToSlash(filepath.Join(displayPath, relativePath)),
670: 			path,
671: 			fileInfo,
672: 		)
673: 		if err != nil {
674: 			return err
675: 		}
676: 
677: 		files = append(files, file)
678: 
679: 		return nil
680: 	})
681: 	if err != nil {
682: 		return nil, err
683: 	}
684: 
685: 	sort.Slice(files, func(i, j int) bool {
686: 		return files[i].filePath < files[j].filePath
687: 	})
688: 
689: 	return files, nil
690: }
691: 
692: func loadStagedLocalFile(
693: 	filePath,
694: 	resolvedPath string,
695: 	fileInfo os.FileInfo,
696: ) (stagedLocalFile, error) {
697: 	content, err := os.ReadFile(resolvedPath) //nolint:gosec
698: 	if err != nil {
699: 		return stagedLocalFile{}, err
700: 	}
701: 	if len(content) > maxConfigMapFileBytes {
702: 		return stagedLocalFile{}, fmt.Errorf(
703: 			"file %q is %d bytes, larger than the supported ConfigMap file limit of %d bytes",
704: 			resolvedPath,
705: 			len(content),
706: 			maxConfigMapFileBytes,
707: 		)
708: 	}
709: 
710: 	mode := fileModeRead
711: 	if fileInfo.Mode()&0o111 != 0 {
712: 		mode = fileModeExecute
713: 	}
714: 
715: 	return stagedLocalFile{
716: 		filePath: filepath.ToSlash(filePath),
717: 		mode:     mode,
718: 		content:  content,
719: 	}, nil
720: }
721: 
722: func replaceClabPathVariables(sourcePath, nodeName, topologyLabDir string) string {
723: 	labName := filepath.Base(topologyLabDir)
724: 	nodeDir := ""
725: 	if topologyLabDir != "" && nodeName != "" {
726: 		nodeDir = filepath.Join(topologyLabDir, nodeName)
727: 	}
728: 
729: 	replacer := strings.NewReplacer(
730: 		clabDirVar, topologyLabDir,
731: 		clabLabNameVar, labName,
732: 		nodeDirVar, nodeDir,
733: 		nodeNameVar, nodeName,
734: 	)
735: 
736: 	return replacer.Replace(sourcePath)
737: }
738: 
739: func stageLocalFile(
740: 	configMaps map[string]*stagedConfigMap,
741: 	topologyName,
742: 	nodeName string,
743: 	file stagedLocalFile,
744: ) error {
745: 	return stageConfigMapFile(
746: 		configMaps,
747: 		topologyName,
748: 		file.filePath,
749: 		nodeName,
750: 		file.filePath,
751: 		file.mode,
752: 		file.content,
753: 	)
754: }
755: 
756: func stageConfigMapFile(
757: 	configMaps map[string]*stagedConfigMap,
758: 	topologyName,
759: 	identity,
760: 	nodeName,
761: 	filePath,
762: 	mode string,
763: 	content []byte,
764: ) error {
765: 	if len(content) > maxConfigMapFileBytes {
766: 		return fmt.Errorf(
767: 			"file %q is %d bytes, larger than the supported ConfigMap file limit of %d bytes",
768: 			filePath,
769: 			len(content),
770: 			maxConfigMapFileBytes,
771: 		)
772: 	}
773: 	identity = filepath.ToSlash(filepath.Clean(identity))
774: 	configMap, ok := configMaps[identity]
775: 	if ok {
776: 		if existing, _ := configMap.content("file"); !bytes.Equal(existing, content) {
777: 			return fmt.Errorf("staged file path %q has conflicting content", filePath)
778: 		}
779: 	} else {
780: 		configMap = &stagedConfigMap{
781: 			name:       stagedFileConfigMapName(topologyName, identity),
782: 			data:       map[string]string{},
783: 			binaryData: map[string][]byte{},
784: 		}
785: 		if isTextContent(content) {
786: 			configMap.data["file"] = string(content)
787: 		} else {
788: 			configMap.binaryData["file"] = content
789: 		}
790: 		configMaps[identity] = configMap
791: 	}
792: 
793: 	for _, mount := range configMap.mounts {
794: 		if mount.nodeName == nodeName && mount.filePath == filePath {
795: 			return nil
796: 		}
797: 	}
798: 
799: 	configMap.mounts = append(configMap.mounts, stagedConfigMapMount{
800: 		nodeName:      nodeName,
801: 		filePath:      filePath,
802: 		configMapName: configMap.name,
803: 		configMapPath: "file",
804: 		mode:          mode,
805: 	})
806: 
807: 	return nil
808: }
809: 
810: func stagedFileConfigMapName(topologyName, filePath string) string {
811: 	digest := sha256.Sum256([]byte(filepath.ToSlash(filepath.Clean(filePath))))
812: 
813: 	return safeKubernetesName(topologyName, "file", hex.EncodeToString(digest[:])[:16])
814: }
815: 
816: func collectStagedConfigMaps(configMapGroups ...map[string]*stagedConfigMap) []stagedConfigMap {
817: 	configMaps := []stagedConfigMap{}
818: 
819: 	for _, configMapGroup := range configMapGroups {
820: 		nodeNames := make([]string, 0, len(configMapGroup))
821: 		for nodeName := range configMapGroup {
822: 			nodeNames = append(nodeNames, nodeName)
823: 		}
824: 		sort.Strings(nodeNames)
825: 
826: 		for _, nodeName := range nodeNames {
827: 			configMap := configMapGroup[nodeName]
828: 			sort.Slice(configMap.mounts, func(i, j int) bool {
829: 				return configMap.mounts[i].filePath < configMap.mounts[j].filePath
830: 			})
831: 			configMaps = append(configMaps, *configMap)
832: 		}
833: 	}
834: 
835: 	return configMaps
836: }
837: 
838: func setTopologyFilesFromConfigMaps(
839: 	topology *unstructured.Unstructured,
840: 	configMaps []stagedConfigMap,
841: ) error {
842: 	if len(configMaps) == 0 {
843: 		return nil
844: 	}
845: 
846: 	filesFromConfigMap := map[string]any{}
847: 	for _, configMap := range configMaps {
848: 		for _, mount := range configMap.mounts {
849: 			nodeFiles, _ := filesFromConfigMap[mount.nodeName].([]any)
850: 			nodeFiles = append(nodeFiles, map[string]any{
851: 				"filePath":      mount.filePath,
852: 				"configMapName": mount.configMapName,
853: 				"configMapPath": mount.configMapPath,
854: 				"mode":          mount.mode,
855: 			})
856: 			filesFromConfigMap[mount.nodeName] = nodeFiles
857: 		}
858: 	}
859: 
860: 	deployment, found, err := unstructured.NestedMap(topology.Object, "spec", "deployment")
861: 	if err != nil {
862: 		return err
863: 	}
864: 	if !found {
865: 		deployment = map[string]any{}
866: 	}
867: 
868: 	deployment["filesFromConfigMap"] = filesFromConfigMap
869: 
870: 	return unstructured.SetNestedMap(topology.Object, deployment, "spec", "deployment")
871: }
872: 
873: func (r *Runtime) applyStagedConfigMaps(
874: 	ctx context.Context,
875: 	namespace string,
876: 	topologyName string,
877: 	configMaps []stagedConfigMap,
878: 	maxWorkers uint,
879: ) error {
880: 	if len(configMaps) == 0 {
881: 		return nil
882: 	}
883: 
884: 	workers, clientBurst := r.kubernetesWorkers(maxWorkers)
885: 	warnWorkersExceedBurst("ConfigMap", maxWorkers, clientBurst, len(configMaps))
886: 
887: 	return runWithWorkers(configMaps, workers, func(staged stagedConfigMap) error {
888: 		return r.applyStagedConfigMap(ctx, namespace, topologyName, staged)
889: 	})
890: }
891: 
892: func (r *Runtime) applyStagedConfigMap(
893: 	ctx context.Context,
894: 	namespace string,
895: 	topologyName string,
896: 	staged stagedConfigMap,
897: ) error {
898: 	configMap := stagedConfigMapObject(namespace, topologyName, staged, nil)
899: 
900: 	_, err := r.kubeClient.CoreV1().ConfigMaps(namespace).
901: 		Create(ctx, configMap, metav1.CreateOptions{})
902: 	if apierrors.IsAlreadyExists(err) {
903: 		existing, getErr := r.kubeClient.CoreV1().ConfigMaps(namespace).
904: 			Get(ctx, staged.name, metav1.GetOptions{})
905: 		if getErr != nil {
906: 			return fmt.Errorf("failed to get existing staged ConfigMap %s/%s: %w",
907: 				namespace,
908: 				staged.name,
909: 				getErr,
910: 			)
911: 		}
912: 
913: 		updated := existing.DeepCopy()
914: 		updated.Labels = mergeDesiredMetadata(existing.Labels, configMap.Labels)
915: 		updated.Data = configMap.Data
916: 		updated.BinaryData = configMap.BinaryData
917: 		err = nil
918: 		if !stagedConfigMapsConform(existing, updated) {
919: 			_, err = r.kubeClient.CoreV1().ConfigMaps(namespace).
920: 				Update(ctx, updated, metav1.UpdateOptions{})
921: 		}
922: 	}
923: 	if err != nil {
924: 		return fmt.Errorf("failed to apply staged ConfigMap %s/%s: %w",
925: 			namespace,
926: 			staged.name,
927: 			err,
928: 		)
929: 	}
930: 
931: 	return nil
932: }
933: 
934: func stagedConfigMapsConform(existing, desired *corev1.ConfigMap) bool {
935: 	return apiequality.Semantic.DeepEqual(existing.Labels, desired.Labels) &&
936: 		apiequality.Semantic.DeepEqual(existing.Data, desired.Data) &&
937: 		apiequality.Semantic.DeepEqual(existing.BinaryData, desired.BinaryData)
938: }

940: func stagedConfigMapObject(
941: 	namespace string,
942: 	topologyName string,
943: 	staged stagedConfigMap,
944: 	ownerReferences []metav1.OwnerReference,
945: ) *corev1.ConfigMap {
946: 	// Empty maps are left nil so neither field is serialized when it carries nothing.
947: 	var data map[string]string
948: 	if len(staged.data) != 0 {
949: 		data = staged.data
950: 	}
951: 	var binaryData map[string][]byte
952: 	if len(staged.binaryData) != 0 {
953: 		binaryData = staged.binaryData
954: 	}
955: 
956: 	return &corev1.ConfigMap{
957: 		TypeMeta: metav1.TypeMeta{APIVersion: "v1", Kind: "ConfigMap"},
958: 		ObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{
959: 			Name:            staged.name,
960: 			Namespace:       namespace,
961: 			OwnerReferences: ownerReferences,
962: 			Labels: map[string]string{
963: 				labelApp:           clabernetesAppValue,
964: 				labelTopologyOwner: topologyName,
965: 			},
966: 		},
967: 		Data:       data,
968: 		BinaryData: binaryData,
969: 	}
970: }
